Mr. President,

**[00:31:26.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000709)  
the Philippine Space Port

**[00:31:37.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000715)

So there is a

So, Mr. President,

**[00:32:11.400] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000732)  
they've had rocket launches

**[00:32:12.540] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000733)  
in the past

**[00:32:13.160] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000734)

**[00:32:14.340] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000735)

**[00:32:23.340] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000741)

**[00:32:25.600] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000743)

**[00:32:28.420] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000744)  
So, Mr. President,

**[00:32:29.600] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000745)  
the objective

**[00:32:30.440] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000746)  
of that rocket launch

**[00:32:31.520] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000747)  
in February

**[00:32:32.040] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000748)  
is not simply

**[00:32:32.900] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000749)

**[00:32:34.180] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000750)  
It's to build the people,

**[00:32:35.500] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000751)

**[00:32:36.200] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000752)

**[00:32:37.360] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000753)

**[00:32:38.760] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000754)

**[00:32:42.420] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000758)

**[00:32:43.360] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000759)  
So, para siyang pilot.

**[00:32:44.620] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000760)  
Why do we need

**[00:32:45.360] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000761)

**[00:32:46.920] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000762)  
Oh, Mr. President,

**[00:32:48.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000763)  
I'm the principal sponsor

**[00:32:51.040] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000764)  
and author

**[00:32:51.540] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000765)  
of the Philippine Space Agency.

**[00:32:53.320] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000766)  
So, Mr. President,

**[00:32:54.160] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000767)

**[00:32:54.860] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000768)

**[00:32:56.220] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000769)

**[00:32:58.540] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000770)

**[00:32:59.260] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000771)  
smaller in nature

**[00:33:00.800] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000772)  
and sometimes

**[00:33:01.720] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000773)

**[00:33:03.520] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000774)  
They include

**[00:33:04.160] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000775)  
experiments

**[00:33:07.860] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000776)  
that the Philippine Space Agency

**[00:33:10.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000777)

**[00:33:11.860] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000778)  
But, Mr. Chair,

**[00:33:12.640] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000779)  
you said this was

**[00:33:13.340] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000780)

**[00:33:14.140] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000781)

**[00:33:15.280] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000782)

**[00:33:16.320] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000783)  
Yes, Mr. President.

**[00:33:16.940] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000784)  
So, they're ramping up

**[00:33:17.980] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000785)

**[00:33:19.560] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000786)  
But, Mr. President...

**[00:33:20.660] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000787)  
What is the purpose?

**[00:33:21.440] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000788)  
So, you launch the rocket

**[00:33:22.700] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000789)  
and you acquire

**[00:33:24.720] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000790)  
satellite capabilities

**[00:33:26.780] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000791)  
which you can actually

**[00:33:28.000] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000792)

**[00:33:28.700] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000793)  
simply

**[00:33:29.140] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000794)

**[00:33:30.100] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000795)

**[00:33:31.880] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000796)  
I don't understand

**[00:33:33.020] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000797)

**[00:33:34.360] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000798)

**[00:33:39.720] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000799)  
Matagal na yan

**[00:33:40.560] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000800)

**[00:33:41.740] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000801)  
Mr. President,

**[00:33:42.520] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000802)  
right now,

**[00:33:43.480] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000803)

**[00:33:44.880] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000804)

**[00:33:46.160] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000805)

**[00:33:47.580] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000806)  
which gives us

**[00:33:48.660] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000807)

**[00:33:49.760] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000808)  
and we have to,

**[00:33:50.840] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000809)  
of course,

**[00:33:51.060] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000810)

**[00:33:53.260] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000811)  
Well, NASA usually

**[00:33:54.260] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000812)

**[00:33:55.320] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000813)

**[00:33:55.540] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000814)

**[00:33:57.060] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000815)  
We have the remote

**[00:33:57.780] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000816)

**[00:33:58.680] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000817)  
which we have to pay for.

**[00:33:59.140] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000818)  
We hardly use it

**[00:34:00.000] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000819)  
and so on.

**[00:34:01.140] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000820)  
So, I'm just curious,

**[00:34:02.660] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000821)  
what additional value

**[00:34:03.880] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000822)

**[00:34:06.560] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000823)  
Yes, Mr. President.

**[00:34:08.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000824)  
So, sovereign access

**[00:34:09.320] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000825)  
to this data.

**[00:34:11.060] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000826)  
These are all, by the way,

**[00:34:12.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000827)  
in the law,

**[00:34:12.720] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000828)

**[00:34:14.280] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-000829)  
It's mandated.

**[00:34:15.380] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000830)  
To develop our own

**[00:34:16.640] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000831)

**[00:34:17.460] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000832)

**[00:34:18.400] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000833)  
And, Mr. President,

**[00:34:19.080] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000834)  
when we were crafting the law,

**[00:34:21.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000835)  
and again,

**[00:34:21.660] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000836)  
I remember talking

**[00:34:22.460] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000837)

**[00:34:24.540] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000838)  
at this time

**[00:34:25.300] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000839)  
that there might be

**[00:34:27.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000840)

**[00:34:27.540] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000841)

**[00:39:12.960] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001059)

**[00:39:14.220] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001060)

**[00:39:16.200] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001061)

**[00:39:17.260] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001062)

**[00:39:18.300] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001063)

**[00:39:35.020] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001075)

**[00:39:46.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001085)

**[00:39:55.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001092)

**[00:39:56.920] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001093)

**[00:39:57.560] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001094)

**[00:44:55.780] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001279)  
So having confirmed

**[00:44:56.800] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001280)  
that there is

**[00:44:57.540] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001281)  
no specific

**[00:44:58.260] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001282)  
budgetary allocation

**[00:44:59.420] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001283)  
either in

**[00:45:00.000] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001284)  
the GAA

**[00:45:00.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001289)  
or the internally generated funds of the BCDA in its corporate operating budget,

**[00:45:06.820] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001290)  
is there any allocation whatsoever for the compensation and relocation of residents

**[00:45:11.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001291)  
who stand to be displaced by the project?

**[00:45:14.900] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001292)  
Yes, Mr. President. Let me confer with them.

**[00:45:20.400] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001293)  
By the way, Mr. President, in the previous hearing we had about PAC Silica,

**[00:45:24.920] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001294)  
I mentioned to them that it is this representation's position

**[00:45:28.560] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001295)  
that nothing should be built, nothing should be done

**[00:45:32.080] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001296)  
if the families, farmers, and IPs in the area are not provided support,

**[00:45:38.940] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001297)  
makausap ng maayos, at makakuha ng buy-in dito po sa pinaplano ng gobyerno.

**[00:45:46.940] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001298)  
So I am also interested to find out, Mr. President,

**[00:45:50.200] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001299)  
if there is a budget for the families that are currently there.

**[00:45:54.300] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001300)  
And since the second hearing has not been scheduled,

**[00:45:57.920] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001301)  
I'm taking this...

**[00:45:58.560] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001302)  
I'm taking this opportunity to find out since notices of eviction apparently

**[00:46:02.660] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001303)  
are requests for quitclaims and waivers of those residing or owning land in the area

**[00:46:10.760] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001304)  
have been issued.

**[00:46:12.980] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001305)  
Meron na daw notice eh of eviction.

**[00:46:16.040] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001306)  
Meron na daw quitclaim and waiver. Kinausap na daw yung mga kapitan.

**[00:46:20.240] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001307)  
Kaya gusto namin malaman, kung wala naman sa budget,

**[00:46:24.880] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001308)  
ano ibigay dun sa mga paaalisin?

**[00:46:28.560] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001309)  
So how much for next year?

**[00:46:41.800] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001310)  
322 million pesos.

**[00:46:47.220] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001310-split-2)  
In the sindagaan. In the indagaan. Ah, sorry, in the... Okay.

**[00:46:52.320] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001310-split-3)  
So there is 322 million pesos allocated for financial assistance.

**[00:46:57.420] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001311)  
This is not...

**[00:46:58.420] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001313)  
particularly in the areas affected by Pax Silica.

**[00:47:01.720] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001314)  
This is in all BCDA areas.

**[00:47:05.460] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001315)  
But if ever there would be assistance to the families there,

**[00:47:09.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001316)  
it would be coming from this budget.

**[00:47:10.460] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001317)  
It is not in the ComReport.

**[00:47:12.320] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001318)  
It is in their corporate funds.

**[00:47:14.300] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001319)  
And it's 320 million, is that correct?

**[00:47:17.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001320)  
322 million, Mr. President.

**[00:47:18.940] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001321)  
322 million.

**[00:47:20.400] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001322)  
And have you conducted a comprehensive inventory of the families,

**[00:47:24.000] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001323)  
farmers, IPs, and other documents directly or indirectly to be displayed?

**[00:47:28.420] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001324)  
to be displaced by the proposed economic zone?

**[00:47:31.380] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001325)  
Yes, Mr. President. They have, Mr. President.

**[00:47:32.960] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001326)  
And how many will be affected?

**[00:47:42.200] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001327)  
Ilan?

**[00:47:47.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001329)  
Families.

**[00:47:48.400] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001330)  
Kasi may binigay ng mga numero nung meeting natin, di ba, Chair?

**[00:47:52.440] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001331)  
Meron silang binibigay ng numbers.

**[00:47:54.560] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001332)  
Pero to be continued daw yung

**[00:47:57.180] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001333)  
pagbibilang.

**[00:47:58.420] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001334)  
Ah, Mr. President, so...

**[00:47:59.500] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001334-split-2)  
Ayun pa rin. Yun pa rin. Walang bago. I know, because during the hearing, nagbigay sila ng numero.

**[00:48:05.480] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001335)  
Lumiit nga, Mr. President, eh. Lumiit. Ngayon, 198 officially from BCDA. But if you remember, in our hearing, it was closer to...

**[00:48:14.220] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001335-split-2)  
400.

**[00:48:15.380] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001335-split-3)  
400?

**[00:48:17.260] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001336)  
And honestly, Mr. President, latest reports have it higher to about 1,000.

**[00:48:21.320] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001337)  
Correct. So, it would be good, Mr. President, if maybe an independent agency like DSWD or NCIP could help us with identifying who are the possible families who will be affected.

**[00:48:34.920] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001338)  
Because we want to make sure that if this initiative does push through, wala pong kababayan na masasagasaan o mapeperwisyo.

**[00:48:43.620] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001339)  
I'm sure you've heard, as well as Senator Padilla and myself, from the IPs, as well as the...

**[00:48:51.320] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001340)  
Residents, those in Tarlac and elsewhere, who are very, very concerned because they've really received notices to vacate and the request to sign quick claims and waivers.

**[00:49:03.920] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001341)  
Has BCDA determined the total amount necessary to compensate these people who will be affected in the loss of their homes, their agricultural land, their livelihood, their crops?

**[00:49:17.900] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001342)  
Meron na kayong estimate kung magkano ang kakailanganin?

**[00:49:20.780] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001343)  
Meron na ba kayo?

**[00:49:21.320] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001344)  
Kaya nung 322 ninyo.

**[00:49:24.420] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001345)  
So, wala pang...

**[00:49:26.160] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001346)  
So, has anybody been given quick claims or anything?

**[00:49:29.900] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001348)  
Mr. President, for the record, BCDA is saying that they're still currently talking to the affected individuals in our hearing.

**[00:49:39.200] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001349)  
And this is, of course, before Mr. Nepomuceno's time.

**[00:49:41.620] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001350)  
We also enjoined the President then not to do press releases without informing the local leaders first.

**[00:49:50.680] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001351)  
Because even the mayor of Capas found out about this initiative on the news.

**[00:49:55.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001352)  
Yes, not even, nobody really talked to him.

**[00:49:59.160] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001353)  
But, Mr. President, for the record, wala naman daw pong pinapapirmang quick claim.

**[00:50:03.300] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001354)  
So, they are denying.

**[00:50:04.660] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001355)  
And it would be good if we can have another hearing.

**[00:50:08.540] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001356)  
And we have evidence to this that any quick claims were provided to any of the families there.

**[00:50:14.640] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001357)  
Are there any identified potential relocation sites for the affected families?

**[00:50:20.680] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001358)  
Yes, possibly.

**[00:50:24.460] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001359)  
But, Mr. President, may I also remind everyone that this area is three times the size of BGC.

**[00:50:31.800] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001360)  
That's right.

**[00:50:32.400] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001361)  
So, whether these are 300, 500, or 1,000 families, surely there needs to be buy-in, there needs to be consent,

**[00:50:42.500] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001362)  
and there needs to be a good area for them, no?

**[00:50:45.160] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001363)  
Or even to carve out their area.

**[00:50:47.220] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001364)  
Correct.

**[00:50:47.360] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001365)  
Because their area is so huge.

**[00:50:48.660] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001366)  
We need to derive informed consent.

**[00:50:50.680] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001367)  
There's cost to develop these areas, to provide them with adequate housing, basic utilities, livelihood opportunities, and so on.

**[00:50:59.280] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001368)  
I also don't know how you can compensate the burial grounds and the ancestors of some of our IPs.

**[00:51:06.560] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001369)  
Mr. President, that was brought up in the hearing.

**[00:51:07.880] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001370)  
I suggested we have a burial ground in the middle of this area.

**[00:51:11.780] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001371)  
Why not?

**[00:51:12.260] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001372)  
Di ba?

**[00:51:12.480] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001373)  
I mean, kung yun po yung ayaw hoon nating makatapak sa mga karapatan ng ating IPs.

**[00:51:18.800] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001374)  
That area.

**[00:51:20.680] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001375)  
We will not match up to the total area of three times the size of BGC or the size of Malabon.

**[00:51:27.240] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001376)  
And Mr. President, ito naman, kausap ko rin yung predecessor ni Mr. Nepomoceno and of course the board of BCDA.

**[00:51:34.460] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001377)  
Kailangan rin po natin ng transparency dito.

**[00:51:36.920] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001378)  
Sa mga hearing po natin, nagbabago-bago rin kung ano ba talaga yung itatayo po dito sa area na to.

**[00:51:43.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001379)  
Nagsimula yan with data center, marami yung umalma, kasama na po tayo.

**[00:51:46.920] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001380)  
And then suddenly in the hearing, wala nang data center.

**[00:51:49.760] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001381)  
Naging manufacturing.

**[00:51:50.680] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001382)  
Pero hindi rin malinaw kung anong klaseng manufacturing.

**[00:51:54.920] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001383)  
Nakumpisa nga yung manufacturing dun sa critical minerals, di ba?

**[00:52:00.060] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001384)  
So Mr. President, I suggested this to the board and of course to the predecessor of Mr. Nepomoceno.

**[00:52:05.740] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001385)  
Mahalaga na malinaw sa taong bayan.

**[00:52:07.800] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001386)  
Hindi lang yung mga taong nandun, pero lahat po tayo, kailangan malinaw ano ba talaga itong pinapasok natin.

**[00:52:14.380] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001387)  
So at least it's clear to us what the deals are, what the negotiations are.

**[00:52:19.580] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001388)  
At malaman natin.

**[00:52:21.220] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001389)  
Ano ba itong pinapasok po natin dito with the United States?

**[00:52:25.880] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001390)  
Yes, but given that this is a budget hearing, in the absence of corresponding budgetary allocation,

**[00:52:33.360] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001391)  
who will shoulder the cost that will inevitably be incurred by the transfer of these people,

**[00:52:38.880] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001392)  
their relocation, the restitution of their livelihoods?

**[00:52:42.240] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001393)  
Is it going to be the Philippine government or is it the U.S. or whoever the occupants will be,

**[00:52:49.120] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001394)  
the private and commercial enterprises?

**[00:52:59.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001395)  
So Mr. President, I've been informed here that any possible locator will be shouldering these expenses.

**[00:53:07.160] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001396)  
So kanya-kanya yun?

**[00:53:08.600] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001397)  
Yes, the locators will be funding the development of the area.

**[00:53:12.860] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001398)  
The locators will have to fund these financial support programs.

**[00:53:16.660] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001399)  
But of course, BCDA is in charge.

**[00:53:19.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001400)  
of social preparation and all of the initial talks.

**[00:53:24.180] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001401)  
But yung pondo po will be charged to the private sector.

**[00:53:28.060] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001402)  
Dahil kasi, isang bagay yung gastos.

**[00:53:35.100] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001403)  
Pero sa kabila nun, may dapat magagarantiya ng mga karapatan ng mga residents na na-displaced.

**[00:53:43.300] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001404)  
Lalong-lalo na yung mga hamak na IP dapat maproteksyonan.

**[00:53:47.480] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001405)  
Meron rin silang karapatan.

**[00:53:49.120] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001406)  
Eh kung yung commercial entity lang ang papasok, sinong lalaban para sa kanila?

**[00:53:54.860] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001407)  
Yes, good question, Mr. President.

**[00:53:56.320] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001408)  
So there's a commitment that BCDA will be handling all of these concerns, no?

**[00:54:03.980] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001409)  
While the locators will be funding the actual physical development of the area.

**[00:54:08.700] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001410)  
So BCDA categorically commits that no resident will be displaced,

**[00:54:14.400] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001411)  
no agricultural land will be cleared, and no community be relocated.

**[00:54:19.120] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001412)  
Until sufficient funds are secured, and their rights are acknowledged and respected.

**[00:54:26.040] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001413)  
Yes, there's a commitment, Mr. President, from BCDA.

**[00:54:29.880] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001414)  
Doon naman sa energy concerns, the proposed hub is projected to require 3 gigawatts of electricity,

**[00:54:37.900] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001415)  
which is about 16% of the entire Luzon grid.

**[00:54:42.180] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001416)  
Do we have a concrete plan?

**[00:54:47.080] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001417)  
Kasi nung una narinig ko, may nagbo-volunteer.

**[00:54:49.120] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001418)  
Nagpo-volunteer na pumasok, magpo-provide ng power producer siya.

**[00:54:53.780] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001419)  
Pagkatapos nun, parang umatras.

**[00:54:57.200] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001420)  
Tapos, how can we produce this huge amount in such a short span of time?

**[00:55:07.820] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001421)  
Mr. President, so galing rin po sa BCDA, nagbabago-bago rin yung porma nito.

**[00:55:14.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001422)  
Okay.

**[00:55:15.040] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001423)  
But yung 3 gigawatts, they admit that this was stated at some point,

**[00:55:19.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001424)  
because this could possibly be the need.

**[00:55:21.300] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001425)  
But they also mentioned that their plan is not to tap into the Luzon grid,

**[00:55:25.440] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001426)  
but to generate the energy on-site.

**[00:55:30.720] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001427)  
Tama. Hirap na hirap na nga yung Luzon grid.

**[00:55:33.300] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001428)  
At yung Visayas, talagang problematic na.

**[00:55:36.520] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001429)  
However, Mr. President, I raised this also, that if that's the case,

**[00:55:42.640] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001430)  
because no power plants are made in 2 years.

**[00:55:45.460] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001431)  
Everything takes about 5 years.

**[00:55:47.540] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001432)  
So, this actually means,

**[00:55:49.240] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001433)  
that the locators will locate there in 5 years.

**[00:55:52.720] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001434)  
Because if, I mean, that's what it means, Mr. President.

**[00:55:56.680] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001435)  
So, gaya po ninyo, paibay ba talaga yung mga press release?

**[00:56:01.460] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001436)  
Paibay ba rin yung mga sinasabi ng mga leaders dito?

**[00:56:04.780] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001437)  
And we call for transparency and a clear plan before we move on towards funding this

**[00:56:12.040] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001438)  
or even going the next step, Mr. President.

**[00:56:14.140] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001439)  
Yung Saudi group na sabi,

**[00:56:17.240] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001440)  
awarded na ng 500,

**[00:56:19.120] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001441)  
megawatt solar project.

**[00:56:21.420] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001442)  
Totoo ba yun?

**[00:56:22.500] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001443)  
Lumabas sa dyaryo yun eh.

**[00:56:30.780] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001444)  
So, you have the 500 megawatt project na solar?

**[00:56:34.560] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001445)  
So, there is a, it's prior to the Paxilica announcement.

**[00:56:37.640] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001446)  
Ah, okay.

**[00:56:38.420] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001447)  
That's 500 megawatts by the...

**[00:56:42.840] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001448)  
5 years daw.

**[00:56:44.020] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001449)  
It's outside of the Paxilica...

**[00:56:46.040] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001450)  
Ah, outside of the Paxilica. Walang connection yun.

**[00:56:49.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001451)  
Wala daw po.

**[00:56:49.800] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001452)  
Kasi parang nung nasa dyaryo na, parang kinonect sila.

**[00:56:54.100] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001453)  
So, I think this isn't the only solar or other energy project in the pipeline.

**[00:57:02.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001454)  
Pero 5 years tulad ng nasabi ninyo.

**[00:57:05.420] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001455)  
Pero in the meantime, BCDA should guarantee there will be no power shortages or electricity

**[00:57:10.700] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001456)  
or electric price hikes in the Luzon grid.

**[00:57:16.160] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001457)  
Hindi ba?

**[00:57:16.640] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001458)  
Kasi,

**[00:57:18.080] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001459)  
ah,

**[00:57:18.460] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001460)  
papasok tayo nito,

**[00:57:19.800] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001461)  
mahihirapan pala yung buong Luzon.

**[00:57:22.180] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001462)  
Meron bang, ah,

**[00:57:23.780] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001463)  
makukuha tayong panghako galing sa BCDA na hindi naman tayo mape-pervision ng todo-todo?

**[00:57:30.740] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001464)  
Mr. President,

**[00:57:32.180] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001465)  
I've been informed by the President that all projects have to pass through DOE,

**[00:57:37.600] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001466)  
NGCP,

**[00:57:39.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001467)  
and definitely,

**[00:57:41.180] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001468)  
ah,

**[00:57:42.580] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001469)  
an ERC.

**[00:57:43.380] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001470)  
Siguro naman,

**[00:57:45.620] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001471)  
hindi kayo gagawa ng proyekto na makaka-apekto sa...

**[00:57:48.460] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001472)  
kabuuhan ng Luzon.

**[00:57:49.600] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001473)  
But this is a commitment, of course,

**[00:57:51.680] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001474)  
that they will have to go through the proper regulations

**[00:57:54.380] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001475)  
and with the proper understanding that this project is self-generated,

**[00:57:59.360] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001476)  
the energy is self-generated.

**[00:58:01.160] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001477)  
On the record, Mr. President,

**[00:58:02.700] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001478)  
yan ang binanggit ni BCDA.

**[00:58:04.680] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001479)  
Okay, thank you for that.

**[00:58:06.160] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001480)  
And the other issue, of course, is water.

**[00:58:09.420] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001481)  
Dahil na-anunsyo nung mga

**[00:58:12.820] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001482)  
nagpa-package ng Paxilica

**[00:58:15.780] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001483)  
that it will require 65 million,

**[00:58:18.300] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001484)  
to 90 million liters of water per day.

**[00:58:23.060] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001485)  
Is this still the BCDA's estimate?

**[00:58:34.520] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001486)  
Ito ba yung sinasabing yung Kuan?

**[00:58:36.800] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001487)  
Magko-construct, mag-surface water?

**[00:58:39.340] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001488)  
Ah, what project is that?

**[00:58:42.760] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001489)  
So there is a current joint venture

**[00:58:45.840] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001490)  
of a Korean firm, K-Water,

**[00:58:48.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001491)  
which is outside of the Paxilica area.

**[00:58:52.460] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001492)  
Tama ba po? Yes.

**[00:58:53.940] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001493)  
And the production of this

**[00:58:57.520] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001494)  
is 65 to 90 million liters per day.

**[00:59:01.500] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001495)  
So there might have been confusion about...

**[00:59:04.560] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001496)  
Ito ba yung Kuan?

**[00:59:05.540] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001497)  
Yung sinasabi na surface water harvest and impoundment?

**[00:59:09.840] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001498)  
Ito yung surface water? Ito na yun?

**[00:59:11.900] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001499)  
Mr. President, ang ibig po palang sabihin is that

**[00:59:14.760] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001500)  
if ever may needs yung area,

**[00:59:17.160] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001501)  
dito po kukunin.

**[00:59:18.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001502)  
And it is a new project.

**[00:59:19.700] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001503)  
Right.

**[00:59:20.200] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001504)  
And who's going to underwrite this big effort?

**[00:59:27.100] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001505)  
Sino magbabayad niyan?

**[00:59:28.620] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001506)  
It's a PPP with a Korean company.

**[00:59:31.820] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001507)  
Yes, but PPP can take many forms.

**[00:59:35.140] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001508)  
Ano ang...

**[00:59:36.760] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001509)  
Ano ang...

**[00:59:37.520] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001510)  
And Maynilad ba yan?

**[00:59:39.060] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001511)  
So it's a joint venture between a Korean firm and Maynilad.

**[00:59:44.660] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001512)  
And it's building a facility which will produce...

**[00:59:46.500] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001513)  
Lakuha yung Maynilad.

**[00:59:47.700] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001514)  
65 million to 90 million liters per day.

**[00:59:51.380] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001515)  
So may gastos rin tayo.

**[00:59:52.580] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001516)  
And it's awarded already, original proponent status.

**[00:59:56.040] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001517)  
But this was done before the PacSilica initiative.

**[00:59:59.420] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001518)  
Yes.

**[01:00:00.000] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001519)  
It's 120 million, which was supposed to, liters a day, which was supposed to augment the shortages in Luzon.

**[01:00:07.520] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001520)  
Wala pa dito yung packed silica na 65 to 90.

**[01:00:11.260] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001521)  
No, so the production is 65 to 90.

**[01:00:13.840] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001522)  
No, the production is 120 a day.

**[01:00:16.840] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001523)  
Pero yung 120 na yun, pupunuin yung kakulangan ng tubig sa Luzon grid without packed silica pa.

**[01:00:24.620] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001524)  
Pag may packed silica, may additional 65 to 90.

**[01:00:28.500] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001525)  
The need rises, no?

**[01:00:31.240] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001526)  
Siyempre.

**[01:00:35.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001527)  
Okay, so they plan to scale up all of these utilities, Mr. President, both in terms of energy and water consumption.

**[01:00:43.860] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001528)  
Marami kasi nagtatanong kung talaga yung Bangot, O'Donnell, Abacan Rivers, kaya-kaya niyan,

**[01:00:50.420] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001529)  
i-supply lahat ng water requirements ng packed silica sa hinaharap nating Super El Nino.

**[01:00:58.500] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001530)  
Made by?

**[01:01:01.720] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001531)  
Okay, based on the feasibility studies of this joint venture, according to BCDA, kakayaanin naman daw po.

**[01:01:10.740] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001532)  
But definitely, now that we're taking this up here, I guess in the hearings, we will have to delve deeper into this to find out.

**[01:01:18.920] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001533)  
Sana magkaroon ng second hearing. Wala pa kasi yan. Tagal na eh.

**[01:01:22.400] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001534)  
Gusto kong malaman kung meron na talagang impact na pinag-aralan nationally.

**[01:01:28.500] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001535)  
Ang irrigation, ang NIA, ang National Water Resources Board, kung i-divert lahat ng tubig nitong ilog natin,

**[01:01:36.500] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001536)  
anong mangyayari sa existing agricultural irrigation systems, downstream communities, all the existing and overarching water rights?

**[01:01:47.720] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001537)  
Kasi alam naman natin, heavily populated itong area na ito, higit sa lahat, eto na yung rice basket ng buong Pilipinas.

**[01:01:55.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001538)  
Yes, definitely.

**[01:01:56.880] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001539)  
If I'm not mistaken, the...

**[01:01:58.500] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001540)  
Was that the Economic Affairs Committee? May I ask the help of Senate President Gatchalian, that hearing that we had on Pax Inca?

**[01:02:07.740] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001541)  
Andaming, andaming committee involved.

**[01:02:09.000] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001542)  
Yes, I think it was the...

**[01:02:10.580] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001543)  
Senator Raffi on Pax Inca.

**[01:02:12.240] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001544)  
I think Environment, the Committee on Environment.

**[01:02:15.620] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001545)  
Committee on Environment.

**[01:02:16.680] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001546)  
So, I'm sure, Mr. President, we can ask for a second hearing because things are moving.

**[01:02:24.440] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001547)  
Yeah, we actually created a committee of a whole.

**[01:02:27.340] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001548)  
Ah, okay.

**[01:02:28.500] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001549)  
Because the Pax Inca project is multidimensional.

**[01:02:33.100] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001550)  
It has economic consequences, it has environmental consequences, it has legal consequences.

**[01:02:38.800] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001551)  
So, we created a committee of a whole.

**[01:02:40.680] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001552)  
So, we will support, Mr. President, the convening of this committee of the whole at the proper time

**[01:02:45.740] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001553)  
so we can have transparency with regard to what it is exactly that we're talking about, Mr. President.

**[01:02:52.800] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001554)  
So, I just looked at my notes.

**[01:02:54.360] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001555)  
It appears that the proposed K-water and Manila...

**[01:02:58.500] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001556)  
The joint project involves 14.8 billion pesos with large infrastructure inputs.

**[01:03:07.780] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001557)  
Is any part of this projected capacity earmarked already for Pax Inca?

**[01:03:15.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001558)  
No.

**[01:03:16.060] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001559)  
Wala pa.

**[01:03:16.800] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001560)  
Kasi hindi pa maliwanag, no?

**[01:03:18.300] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001561)  
So, this joint project with K-water preceded Pax Inca.

**[01:03:23.580] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001562)  
Yes.

**[01:03:23.820] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001563)  
So, wala pa yun.

**[01:03:24.640] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001564)  
It's initially made for the needs of New Clark City.

**[01:03:28.060] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001565)  
Siguro.

**[01:03:28.500] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001566)  
So, yung mahihingi ko na lamag, e garantiya mula sa BCDA na walang tahanang Pilipino na magkukulang ng tubig.

**[01:03:40.960] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001567)  
At saka lahat ng magsasaka ay hindi mawawalaan ng akses sa kanilang irigasyon.

**[01:03:48.760] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001568)  
Pwede bang hingin ang pangako ninyo kasi marami pang nangangamba?

**[01:03:53.480] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001569)  
So, yes, the answer is yes.

**[01:03:58.500] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001570)  
But we've also been informed here that if the plans of New Clark City for water and electricity come to pass,

**[01:04:07.240] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001571)  
there should be an excess even for the neighboring areas.

**[01:04:12.080] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001572)  
Of course, yan yung plano, Mr. President.

**[01:04:14.480] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001573)  
And I think the proper venue would be the Committee on the Whole to go into detail about all of these issues being raised also.

**[01:04:21.040] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001574)  
Yes, which is why we need to get that scheduled impeachment or budget.

**[01:04:25.180] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001575)  
in, uh, um, [unclear] aside.

**[01:04:30.420] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001601)  
So, BCDA provides that guarantee, and I hope no Filipino taxpayer will be required to subsidize the water requirements of PacSilica,

**[01:04:39.920] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001602)  
which should, instead of burdening us, help us develop our manufacturing capability.

**[01:04:46.200] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-001603)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:04:46.960] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001604)  
Thank you, Mr. President.

**[01:04:48.340] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001605)

**[01:04:54.520] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001606)  
And hopefully, Mr. President, the Senate can be the venue.

**[01:04:58.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001607)  
For transparency and to really share with the people, ano ba talaga yung mga nakaplano dito, no?

**[01:05:04.980] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001608)  
And marami pong concerns, no?

**[01:05:07.220] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001609)

**[01:05:14.280] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001610)  
And clarity, I think, will help people understand better kung dapat ba itong masuporta o hindi.

**[01:05:20.920] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001611)  
And we have asked, even from the first hearing, our friends from BCDA, and BOI, si Yusek Rodolfo,

**[01:05:28.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001613)

**[01:05:35.680] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001614)  
Because until today, Mr. President, pabago-bago rin yung information, no?

**[01:05:39.520] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001615)  
So, thank you very much to the gentle lady from Ilocos Norte.

**[01:05:42.760] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001616)

**[01:05:45.940] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001617)

Thank you to the good sponsor.

**[01:05:49.780] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001619)  
And definitely, once we convene the committee of the whole, we will request the different agencies to give us full,

**[01:05:58.140] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001620)

**[01:06:04.820] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001621)  
Alright?

**[01:13:14.660] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001720)  
Just a

**[01:13:15.560] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001721)

**[01:13:16.780] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001722)

**[01:13:21.700] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001724)  
National Electrification

**[01:13:23.800] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001725)  
Administration sa NEA.

**[01:13:26.140] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001726)  
Mr. President,

**[01:13:28.160] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001727)  
we continue to receive complaints

**[01:13:29.860] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001728)

**[01:13:33.720] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001730)

**[01:13:36.080] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001731)  
regarding frequent brownouts.

**[01:13:39.320] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001732)  
In fact, dalawang

**[01:13:40.300] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001733)  
beses po akong pumunta sa Bukidnon,

**[01:13:43.060] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001734)

**[01:13:44.800] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001735)  
at sa Pangantukan

**[01:13:46.460] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001736)  
itong nakaraang linggo

**[01:13:48.060] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001737)  
at pagpunta ko doon, dalawang beses rin

**[01:13:50.060] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001738)  
pong naranasan yung brownout.

**[01:13:52.380] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001739)  
Doon, nagkaroon naman po ng

**[01:13:54.660] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001740)  
explanation po

**[01:13:56.140] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001741)  
ang

**[01:13:59.080] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001742)  
FIBECO

**[01:14:01.280] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001743)  
na ito po'y

**[01:14:03.540] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001744)  
dahil sa

**[01:14:05.840] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001745)  
nagkaroon ng glitch sa

**[01:14:10.100] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001746)  
NGCP, no?

**[01:14:12.300] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001747)  
Sa supply. Pero

**[01:14:14.060] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001748)  
bakit naman nagkataon na sabay

**[01:14:15.900] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001749)  
na dalawang beses? Maharing

**[01:14:18.000] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001750)  
siguro hindi ko kasama yung

**[01:14:19.960] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001751)  
majority floor leader, nagkataon na

**[01:14:22.320] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001752)  
bibiro lang po.

**[01:14:24.220] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001753)  
Nakakalungkot po na

**[01:14:26.140] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001754)  
ang mga ganitong pangyayari

**[01:14:27.800] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001755)  
dahil lalo na ngayon na

**[01:14:29.640] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001756)  
mainit ang panahon, marami pong

**[01:14:31.600] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001757)  
naapektuhan sa

**[01:14:32.900] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001758)  
unreliable electricity,

**[01:14:36.660] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001759)  
apektado po ang skwelahan,

**[01:14:38.700] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001760)  
mga ospital, nasisira

**[01:14:40.200] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001761)  
ang mga appliances,

**[01:14:41.760] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001762)  
apektado rin pong negosyo

**[01:14:43.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001763)  
at maging ang turismo dahil marami

**[01:14:45.740] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001764)  
pong mga tourist destination sa

**[01:14:47.560] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001765)  
Davao Oriental at sa

**[01:14:49.700] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001766)  
Bukidnon.

**[01:14:52.340] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001767)  
Domino effect po ito

**[01:14:53.640] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001768)  
tuwing merong mga brownout.

**[01:14:56.140] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001769)  
Kawawa po ang ating mga kababayan

**[01:14:57.980] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001770)  
natin na nagbabayad naman ang tamang

**[01:14:59.800] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001771)  
rate.

**[01:15:00.940] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001772)  
Pero napeperwisyo.

**[01:15:02.160] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001773)  
Sa totoo lamang po, willing naman po magbayad yung mga kababayan natin basta mabigyan lang po sila ng maayos na servisyo.

**[01:15:10.060] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001774)  
Pero basta, maayos na servisyo po ang iniexpect ng ating mga kababayan.

**[01:15:15.960] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001775)  
I call on the concerned electric cooperatives, Davao Oriental Electric Cooperative, Doreco in Davao Oriental,

**[01:15:23.700] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001776)  
and First Bukidnon Electric Cooperative in Bukidnon to improve your services.

**[01:15:30.000] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001777)  
I want to remind you that the grant of franchise is not a right but a mere privilege.

**[01:15:35.860] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001778)  
At nananawagan rin po tayo sa Department of Energy.

**[01:15:40.160] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001779)  
Kaya nga po, tinawag po itong Departamento ng Energy.

**[01:15:43.980] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001780)  
Klarong-klaro naman po yan.

**[01:15:46.220] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001781)  
Under Republic Act 7638, DOE is mainly responsible for energy policymaking, energy security, electricity supply,

**[01:15:59.340] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001782)  
consumer protection and monitoring, among others.

**[01:16:03.400] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001783)  
At sa NEA naman, kayo rin po ang responsible sa pag-supervise at monitoring sa performance ng electric cooperatives.

**[01:16:11.980] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001784)  
Sabi nga nila, you cannot compel the NGCP dahil private naman po ito.

**[01:16:19.700] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001785)  
But siguro naman, pwede nyo naman po silang pakiusapan.

**[01:16:24.060] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001786)  
At ako po yung nakikiusap rin po sa inyo.

**[01:16:26.000] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001787)  
Sana po'y matutukan nyo ito at gawan ng paraan ang problema sa

**[01:16:30.000] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001788)  
outdated cables. Decades old na po itong mga ito.

**[01:16:34.280] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001789)  
Kaya kulang po ang daloy ng kuryente.

**[01:16:37.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001790)  
Yun po ang rason sa Davao Oriental.

**[01:16:39.660] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001791)  
Dahil malayo po ito, dulo po ito yung Davao Oriental.

**[01:16:42.520] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001792)  
At taga doon rin po ako.

**[01:16:45.240] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001793)  
Sana po itutukan at kalampagin nyo rin po sana ang NGCP para matugunan ang pangangailangan sa mga area na ito.

**[01:16:52.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001794)  
At monitor nyo rin po ang mga electric cooperatives.

**[01:16:55.460] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001795)  
Ako rin naman po, importante sa akin yung trabaho.

**[01:16:58.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001796)  
Ng ating mga kababayan, lalong-lalo na po yung mga mahirap nating kababayan na nagtatrabaho lang po at may natatanggap na sweldo mula sa electric cooperatives.

**[01:17:08.580] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001797)  
Yun po ang aking prioridad.

**[01:17:10.800] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001798)  
Huwag po silang mawalan ng trabaho.

**[01:17:14.600] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001799)  
Ang gusto lang po natin, magtulong-tulong tayo para magkaroon ng mas affordable at reliable na supply ng kuryente sa ating mga kababayan.

**[01:17:23.120] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001800)  
Hindi lang po sa Davao Oriental at sa Bukidnon, kundi pati na rin po sa buong bansa.

**[01:17:27.820] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001801)  
And I'm sure alam po ng Department of Energy, yan po ang naging malaking problema po sa ngayon.

**[01:17:34.900] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001802)  
Importante po sa akin ang trabaho, pero pag may brownout naman po, apektado yung negosyo, apektado yung turismo, apektado rin po ang trabaho ng ating mga kababayan.

**[01:17:46.160] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001803)  
So pakibalansin na lang po ang lahat at matugunan sana ito ng Department of Energy.

**[01:17:52.540] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001804)  
Kaya nga po tinawag kayo na Department of Energy.

**[01:17:55.640] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001805)  
Pakiusap lang natin.

**[01:17:56.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001806)  
Kung tutukan niyo po ang energy issue or problem sa ating bansa.

**[01:18:03.260] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001807)  
Yun lang po, Mr. President, Madam Sponsor, kasi wala na yata rito yung inyong NEA Administrator, parang anniversary nila, no?

**[01:18:14.820] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001808)  
So umalis na po yata siya.

**[01:18:16.100] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001809)  
So ayoko na lang pong tanongin yung kapwa ko, Senador, since nandito naman po itong mga officials.

**[01:18:25.140] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001810)  
Nakikiusap po ako sa inyo.

**[01:18:26.460] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001811)  
Alang-alang lang po sa ating mga kababayan na apektado po dito sa problema sa brownout.

**[01:18:33.260] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001812)  
Maraming salamat po, Mr. President. Magandang umaga.

**[01:18:36.800] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001813)  
Thank you, Mr. President.

**[01:18:38.180] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001814)  
Mr. President, since Bukidnon was mentioned, when it first happened, I intervened.

**[01:18:45.680] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001815)  
Nagpadala po ako ng sulat sa Fibeco kasi yung district area na yun is Fibeco.

**[01:18:50.120] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001816)  
So nagpadala ako ng sulat. They gave a lengthy explanation.

**[01:18:52.640] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001817)  
I think one was...

**[01:18:55.340] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001818)  
Yun nga, there was an outing.

**[01:18:56.460] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001819)  
It's from NGCP.

**[01:18:58.480] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001820)  
But it was quick. I think it was only like 20 minutes or something.

**[01:19:02.820] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001821)  
Natimingan lang po na nagsispeech si Sen. Bong Go noon na brownout.

**[01:19:07.340] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001822)  
Twice po. Dalawang beses.

**[01:19:08.820] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001823)  
Dalawang beses. Sabi ko. Pero hindi kami nagpa-brownout.

**[01:19:12.240] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001824)  
Kasi kaibigan ko yan.

**[01:19:13.820] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001825)  
Hindi kami nag-brownout.

**[01:19:15.100] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001826)  
Siguro kung sinamahan mo ako doon na majority floor leader, baka hindi po nagka-brownout.

**[01:19:20.040] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001827)  
Babantayan kanila na hindi magka-brownout doon.

**[01:19:22.760] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001828)  
Alam mo na, iba yung suspect ko na nagpa-brownout.

**[01:19:25.540] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001829)  
Pero ayaw mo maniwala.

**[01:19:27.860] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001830)  
Wala naman siguro. Wala naman siguro nga ganun.

**[01:19:30.720] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001831)  
Nanonoko lang kita. Nanonoko lang.

**[01:19:32.600] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001832)  
So Mr. President, I know because of the latest of the hour, we just try to lighten the mood.

**[01:19:39.160] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001833)  
But definitely we look into it.

**[01:19:42.260] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001834)  
To my good colleague.

**[01:19:43.960] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001835)  
And I'm sure the Secretary to the NEA will also.

**[01:19:46.300] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001836)  
Yes. Just to respond, the Secretary has already spoken to Sen. Bong Go earlier

**[01:19:53.020] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001837)  
and assured him that...

**[01:19:55.540] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001838)  
She will be sending a team to address this issue.

**[01:20:00.180] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001839)  
I actually also asked the Electric Cooperative to write a formal letter.

**[01:20:05.140] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001840)  
So they did.

**[01:20:06.140] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001841)  
And then I submitted it to Sen. Bong Go.

**[01:20:08.980] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001842)  
But I don't think he was satisfied with the answer.

**[01:20:11.700] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001843)  
Because he said, well of course in the letter there was an outage from the NGCP at the same time.

**[01:20:15.760] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001844)  
There's also problems with yung punong kahoy, di ba?

**[01:20:19.920] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001845)  
Trees, yung tumatama sa poste, sa mga linya.

**[01:20:25.540] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001846)  
And they're clearing it out now.

**[01:20:27.340] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001847)  
But I guess maybe a more comprehensive answer would suffice and satisfy our distinguished colleague.

**[01:20:34.860] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001848)  
Salamat po.

**[01:20:35.540] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001849)  
Hindi lang naman po siguro ito sa Mindanao, sa Bukidnon and Dava Oriental itong problema nito.

**[01:20:40.380] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001850)  
Sa buong Pilipinas po ito.

**[01:20:41.700] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001851)  
And I'm sure alam niyo po yung mga reklamong yan.

**[01:20:44.500] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001852)  
Sana matugunan po ito.

**[01:20:45.860] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001853)  
Sana magkaroon na tayo ng brownout free dito sa ating bansa.

**[01:20:51.140] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-001854)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[01:20:52.300] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001855)  
Thank you, Sen. Bong Go for the short.

**[01:20:55.540] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001856)  
Intervention.

**[01:20:56.840] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-001857)  
Mr. President, we'd now like to recognize the distinguished colleague, Sen. Rizal Tiveros, to propound some questions.

**[01:21:02.780] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001858)  
Recognize Sen. Rizal Tiveros.

**[01:21:14.480] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001859)  
Salamat po, Mr. President. At magandang umaga po sa good sponsor.

**[01:21:19.280] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001860)  
Good morning.

**[01:21:20.620] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001861)  
Originally po, meron akong halos isang dosenang paksa.

**[01:21:25.180] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001862)  
Pero in the interest of time, kakalahatiin ko na lang sa lima, tatlo para sa DOE at dalawa para sa ERC.

**[01:21:34.740] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001863)  
And then, isasabit ko na lang, Mr. President, sa good sponsor yung ibang mga tanong ko para masagot ng department bago tayo dumako sa period of amendments.

**[01:21:45.620] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001864)  
So, una na po.

**[01:21:48.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001865)  
Mahigit isandaang gasolinahan na ang nahuli ng DOE.

**[01:21:55.180] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001866)  
Hindi sumusunod sa itinakdang rollback.

**[01:21:59.180] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001867)  
Yan ang nirereklamo ng mga motorista.

**[01:22:01.940] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001868)  
Pag price increase, ang pilis taasan.

**[01:22:04.940] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001869)  
Pero pag price decrease, ang bagal.

**[01:22:08.020] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001870)  
So, in effect, napipwersang magbayad ng overpriced rates kada litro ang consumers.

**[01:22:14.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001871)  
At kailangang mabawi rin ng mga motorista ang sobrang siningil sa kanila.

**[01:22:20.220] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001872)  
So, may paraan po ba ang DOE para tulungan ang consumers, Mr. President?

**[01:22:25.180] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001873)  
Kasi dapat mabawi nilang sobrang binayad nila.

**[01:22:51.980] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001875)  
Um,

**[01:22:53.180] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001876)  
the result,

**[01:22:55.180] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001877)  
The response, Your Honor, of DOE is that they issue show-cause orders, and then if there is a finding and there's a violation, then they can mete out the penalty. As to some kind of refund or compensation, they're still studying how to go about that.

**[01:23:16.740] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001878)  
All right. Salamat, good sponsor. May kailangan ba ang department sa budget natin para maipatupad ang mga ito?

**[01:23:30.140] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001879)  
No, no. They just have to study how to go about it.

**[01:23:33.420] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001880)  
All right. And ngayon din mismo, may big time na rollback sa presyo ng petrolyo. Paano titiyakin ang department na tama yung presyo sa gas stations nationwide?

**[01:23:46.740] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001881)  
At ano ang magiging parusa sa mga lumabag at nag-overcharge?

**[01:23:52.640] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001882)  
So, according to DOE, under RA 8479 and E0110, DOE is undertaking implementation of the mandated weekly price adjustment and monthly price adjustments,

**[01:24:09.880] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001883)  
implementation of fuel subsidy discount programs. This is 12 pesos per liter on diesel of PUV.

**[01:24:16.740] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001884)  
For a total of 1,000 pesos, 1,800 per week. Discount promos of the private oil companies and nationwide inspection to enforce the proper implementation of the DOE weekly mandated price adjustments.

**[01:24:31.200] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001885)  
Salamat po. May parusa ba kapag mayroon silang ma-identify na hindi sumunod sa rollback?

**[01:24:38.780] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001886)  
And again, sapat ba ang budget ng department para pagbantayan ito?

**[01:24:43.520] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001887)  
Okay.

**[01:24:44.560] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001888)  
So, of course, they go through the process.

**[01:24:46.740] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001889)  
Your Honor, they have a show-cause order, and then when there's a finding, they can impose a penalty and in the worst case scenario, they can cancel the permit.

**[01:24:58.440] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001890)  
All right.

**[01:24:59.540] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001891)  
And tulad nung unang tanong, hindi na nila kailangan ng dagdag pang budget para gawin ang mga steps na ito?

**[01:25:05.600] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001892)  
Hindi na po. It's part of their mandate.

**[01:25:07.760] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001893)  
All right.

**[01:25:09.980] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001894)

**[01:25:14.280] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001895)  
Okay.

**[01:25:15.800] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001896)  
And PNP.

**[01:25:16.740] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001897)  
Okay.

**[01:25:18.240] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001898)  
Good po.

**[01:25:19.380] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001899)  
All right.

**[01:25:20.960] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001900)  
Sunod po.

**[01:25:22.060] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001901)  
Wala pa mga ELNINIO noon, nararanasan natin ang brownout.

**[01:25:27.060] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001902)  
Yellow alerts at red alerts lalo na sa Visayas at Mindanao.

**[01:25:31.820] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001903)  
Dapat po may nakalaan na sa 2027 budget ng DOE para masigurong tuloy-tuloy ang operasyon ng lalo na ating mga ospital at ating mga eskwelahan

**[01:25:44.540] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001904)  
sakaling lumala ang kakulangan.

**[01:25:46.740] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001905)  
ng supply ng kuryente.

**[01:25:49.880] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001906)  
Magkano po ang inilaan natin para dito at ano yung mga kaakibat na contingency plans ng department?

**[01:25:56.100] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001907)  
Mr. President?

**[01:26:16.740] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001908)

**[01:26:35.180] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001909)

**[01:26:44.860] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001910)  
We can give Her Honor a copy.

**[01:26:47.600] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001911)  
Salamat po.

**[01:26:49.020] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001912)  
Ano yung item na nakalaan na sa budget para sa mga ito?

**[01:26:58.300] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001916)  
Okay. So, for example, for the hospitals and schools, it's in their specific budgets.

**[01:27:03.160] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001917)  
Ah, okay. Individually.

**[01:27:05.240] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001918)  
At the same time, good sponsor, Mr. President, sa ganyang mga pagkakataon, mas lalong dapat isama sa pinupondohan natin ang solar at saka battery storage.

**[01:27:16.740] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001919)  
Sa mga public hospitals, public schools, actually, pati sa mga SUCs.

**[01:27:20.860] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001920)  
Tulad ng sinimula na ngayon sa UP Solarization Project.

**[01:27:25.300] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001921)  
At especially kung madalas mag-brown out.

**[01:27:28.760] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001922)  
Nakwenta na rin ba natin kung magkanong kailangan para mais ako tuparan ito sa buong bansa?

**[01:27:38.960] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001923)  
So, actually, yung binanggit ko na NDRR MC Resolution No. 2, 2026,

**[01:27:46.740] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001924)  
kasama dyan, solar PV with battery storage, ang preferred, ano talaga, source, no?

**[01:27:53.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001925)  
And then, yung funding source ng mga LGU would be coming from the local DRRM fund, national DRRM fund, ER-194 fund, climate finance, and other eligible funding sources.

**[01:28:09.040] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001926)  
Alright. So, hindi na po kailangan ng may dagdag pang items sa DOE budget separately.

**[01:28:13.840] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001927)  
Alright. Salamat po.

**[01:28:14.920] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001928)  
Bago yung huling paksakot...

**[01:28:16.740] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001929)  
So, sa last on this particular topic, dun sa budget na meron tayo sa 2027, ilan ang kaya nating maseribisyohan in terms of the schools and hospitals?

**[01:28:46.740] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001932)  
So, actually, as mentioned earlier, this is already in the budgets of the respective institutions.

**[01:28:57.780] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001933)  
So, kung sa school, nasa school, hospital, nasa hospital. So, nandiyan na ho yun, they're supposed to budget for that already.

**[01:29:04.500] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001934)  
Alright. Then, bago nga yung huling paksakot sa DOE, one step back dun sa unang paksa, tungkol sa paanong bababawi ng consumer yung sobrang binayad nila,

**[01:29:14.600] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001935)  
meron din bang pinag-aaralan ng DOE na paraan ng, kung hindi diretsyong refund, pag natabi nung customer yung resibo nila,

**[01:29:26.760] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001936)  
pagbalik dun sa gasolinahan kung saan sobra palang binayad nila noon, may paraan ba na ma-offset yung susunod na babayaran nila?

**[01:29:36.760] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001937)  
Masama yun. Masama yun, your honor, dun sa pinag-aaralan nila.

**[01:29:40.500] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001938)  
Okay. Alright. Salamat.

**[01:29:42.480] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001939)  
Huling paksa ako sa DOE.

**[01:29:44.600] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001940)  
Sir President, good sponsor.

**[01:29:46.460] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001941)  
Noong Setiembre, sinabi ng DOE na target nila ang zero brownout sa Visayas at sa Mindanao ngayong papasok na Disyembre.

**[01:29:56.840] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001942)  
Magagaransya ba ng department?

**[01:30:00.000] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001943)  
na walang brownout at maliwanag ang Pasko ng mga pamilyang Pilipino.

**[01:30:08.320] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001944)  
Makakapagsindi ba ng parol ang bawat pamilyang Pilipino ngayong Pasko?

**[01:30:30.000] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001945)  
So, her honor is correct that that is the objective.

**[01:30:39.500] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001946)  
That is the objective.

**[01:30:40.880] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001947)  
So, what they can do, what is within their power is to make sure that everything that is required to happen is in place.

**[01:30:49.840] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001948)  
So, they're doing all that.

**[01:30:51.100] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001949)  
They're doing all that, making sure that the power plants are functioning up to par, etc., etc.

**[01:30:57.980] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001950)  
So, it goes without saying.

**[01:31:00.000] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001951)  
So, it goes without saying that if in place lahat ito, then the chances of being successful in what we want to achieve, which is zero brownout, will happen.

**[01:31:12.100] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001952)  
Natansya ba nila? Ano yung percentage na chance na ma-achieve nga yung objective na ito by next month sa Visayas at Mindanao?

**[01:31:39.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001956)  
Let's try to understand the reason for the brownouts.

**[01:31:44.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001957)  
Mangyayari yan kung mag-fail yung power plants.

**[01:31:47.880] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001958)  
Kaya, ang gagawin nila, address nila yun.

**[01:31:51.000] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001959)  
Ensure that these power plants can perform.

**[01:31:53.860] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001960)  
So, that is additional reserves.

**[01:31:55.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001961)  
So, reserves is already the backup, di ba?

**[01:31:57.700] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001962)  
I assume mo na na.

**[01:31:59.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001963)  
I think that's part of the, what's the term there?

**[01:32:02.200] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001964)  
Yung proper maintenance and to assure steady supply is may reserve ka.

**[01:32:07.680] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001965)  
It's one thing to say na wala yan, tas wala kang reserve.

**[01:32:10.640] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001966)  
I mean, it won't happen, wala kang reserve.

**[01:32:12.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001967)  
So, that's already being responsible by ensuring that there's additional reserves and incoming generating capacity, intergrid transfers.

**[01:32:20.900] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001968)  
This is all intended to prevent the supply-related interruptions.

**[01:32:26.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001969)  
So, they're doing all that.

**[01:32:29.220] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001970)  
So, I believe, Mr. President, what the good sponsor is saying, that the department is doing all that.

**[01:32:36.140] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001971)  
Lalo na dahil yung department ang nag-set ng target.

**[01:32:38.760] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001972)  
Wala pong nag-impose sa kanila na wala ng brownouts sa Visayas at Mindanao by December.

**[01:32:44.120] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001973)  
So, can they promise na makakasindi ng parolang bawat pamilyang Pilipino?

**[01:32:49.620] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001974)  
Or may percentage ba that they think they will be achieving the self-imposed target by next month?

**[01:32:57.180] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001975)  
So, I think I've answered that.

**[01:32:59.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001976)  
Your Honor, to the best of DOE's ability to respond.

**[01:33:03.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001977)  
So, they can't give us an estimate?

**[01:33:06.560] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001978)  
Kasi, Your Honor, what they can do nga, as I said, and as I will repeat,

**[01:33:10.620] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001979)  
is they are doing everything to ensure that itong power plants are functioning and then may reserve.

**[01:33:19.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001980)  
That's what they can do.

**[01:33:20.580] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001981)  
So, if they do that, then the chances of having brownouts is very minimal.

**[01:33:25.940] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001982)  
They would have given me a percentage.

**[01:33:29.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001983)  
I've heard her Honor's question, but I guess they're not able to really give that.

**[01:33:32.760] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001984)  
But they want to give that assurance as best as they can.

**[01:33:37.400] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001985)  
Well, I guess I'll have to accept that answer from the department.

**[01:33:42.940] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001986)  
Disappointing lang kasi ang ganda sana nung target na naset.

**[01:33:46.360] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001987)  
And I'm sure umasay yung mga kababayan natin sa Visayas at Mindanao by next month.

**[01:33:52.880] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001988)  
And for the record, Mr. President, good sponsor, tayo dito sa Senado, when we set targets,

**[01:33:59.220] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001989)  
and then we ask each other sa mga komite natin, halimbawa, ano na yung progress report,

**[01:34:04.880] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001990)
