**[01:34:08.720] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001991)  
So, I was expecting more from the department.

**[01:34:11.680] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001992)  
And maybe by the time of the period of amendments, they can come up with a better answer for us

**[01:34:17.040] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001993)  
at sa mga kababayan natin, lalo na who are hoping for a brighter Christmas

**[01:34:22.440] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001994)  
than they have experienced in the past months or this whole year, actually.

**[01:34:28.100] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001995)  
Good sponsor.

**[01:34:29.220] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001997)  
Mr. President.

**[01:34:32.460] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-001998)  
Magkano po yung inilaan natin sa 2027 budget para tuluyan na nga matugunan ang problema sa brownout sa Visayas at Mindanao?

**[01:34:59.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-001999)  
So, um, Your Honor, these are private-sector commitments, no? And so what DOE is doing is they're keeping abreast of what's happening, keeping track of the commitments, particularly on battery and barges, kasi diyan ho nanggagaling yung reserve. Kaya nga, like I said,

**[01:35:29.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002000)  
There's a supply, but the backup there is to have the reserve.

**[01:35:34.900] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002001)  
That's very, very important.

**[01:35:36.460] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002002)  
So, they're not literally the ones in charge of those reserves, but they're on top of it,

**[01:35:43.660] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002003)  
ensuring that ito nga, yung batteries and the barges are in place.

**[01:35:49.200] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002004)  
And magkano pong inilaan, Mr. President, para sa barges at sa batteries?

**[01:35:52.540] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002005)  
It's a private endeavor, as I said, Your Honor.

**[01:35:55.080] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002006)  
It's private.

**[01:35:55.600] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002007)  
But is the DOE not able to disclose to us?

**[01:35:59.220] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002008)  
Here in Congress?

**[01:36:00.640] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002009)  
Those are separate contracts.

**[01:36:02.040] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002010)  
Would you like a list of those contracts?

**[01:36:04.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002011)  
Sure, Mr. President.

**[01:36:05.760] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002012)  
Again, we'll ask them to provide, Your Honor.

**[01:36:07.120] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002013)  
Okay, salamat.

**[01:36:08.480] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002014)  
Moving on to my couple of topics para sa ERC.

**[01:36:13.420] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002015)  
Ang pinakamahalagang tungkulin ng ERC ay pagpapababa sa presyo ng kuryente

**[01:36:18.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002016)  
at pagtatanggol sa kapakanan ng mga consumer.

**[01:36:21.380] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002017)  
Sa tulong ng budget na binigay sa commission,

**[01:36:28.380] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002018)  
para sa 2027,

**[01:36:31.220] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002019)  
magkano ang aasahan o target ng ahensya na pagbabasa singil sa consumer

**[01:36:36.820] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002020)  
ng mga distribution utilities next year, Mr. President?

**[01:36:48.060] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002021)  
Can I just ask the question to be repeated?

**[01:36:50.920] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002022)  
Because even if you pass me the answer, I want to hear the question myself.

**[01:36:53.720] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002023)  
Magkano ang aasahan o target ng ERC

**[01:36:57.720] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002024)  
na pagbaba sa

**[01:36:58.380] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002027)  
singil sa consumer ng mga distribution utilities next year.

**[01:37:02.400] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002028)  
Yes, Mr. President.

**[01:37:28.380] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002029)  
So, to be clear, Your Honor, the mandate of ERC is to ensure that the investments in the generation and transmission are bare fruit.

**[01:37:47.000] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002030)  
Because if this is accomplished, then it will naturally happen that the prices will go down.

**[01:37:56.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002031)  
Paso supply and demand lang po yun, Your Honor.

**[01:37:58.180] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002032)  
Kung nandyan yung capacity to provide, then there will be enough supply and the prices will come down at a lower.

**[01:38:08.800] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002033)  
Pag kulang ang supply, edi nagmamahal.

**[01:38:11.560] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002034)  
So, that is the mandate of ERC, to ensure that this is in place.

**[01:38:16.780] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002035)  
Unfortunately, Mr. President, good sponsor, yun na nga yung pangako sa ilalim ng EPIRA at doon na nga dinerive yung ganyang mandate ng ERC.

**[01:38:26.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002036)  
And although there have been numerous investments in the last quarter century at maraming private sector stakeholders ay kumita,

**[01:38:39.840] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002037)  
hindi pa natin na-achieve yung least cost price para sa mga consumers.

**[01:38:47.240] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002038)  
So, hindi purely nag-ooperate yung law of supply and demand dito.

**[01:38:51.620] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002039)  
So, given that hindi pa optimal.

**[01:38:56.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002040)  
Na situation, meron din bang tansya ang ERC kung by how much bababa ang singil,

**[01:39:04.420] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002041)  
pagsingil sa customers ng mga distribution utilities sa 2027.

**[01:39:26.580] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002042)  
The first thing that they are doing to address this concern, which is yung mataas na presyo,

**[01:39:47.020] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002043)  
at yung goal naman is very clear na bumaba sana ang presyo, is addressing the backlog.

**[01:39:52.040] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002044)  
That I know, kasi in the years that I was chairman of the...

**[01:39:56.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002045)  
of the Committee on Energy, that really has been an ongoing concern.

**[01:40:01.980] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002046)  
Like talagang grabe yung backlog.

**[01:40:03.760] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002047)  
So, I remember when the last hearing I had, that's really something that was highlighted by ERC.

**[01:40:11.040] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002048)  
And then second is yung rate reset.

**[01:40:14.120] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002049)  
Tinatrabaho talaga nila yun.

**[01:40:15.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002050)  
Now, as to her honor's specific question, magkano.

**[01:40:21.060] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002051)  
It's difficult to answer because as I said, supply and demand.

**[01:40:24.660] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002052)  
And a lot of things.

**[01:40:26.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002053)  
But going to the price are beyond the control of ERC.

**[01:40:30.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002054)  
And as her honor knows, the peso, like hindi naman...

**[01:40:34.440] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002055)  
I don't think anyone had expected na ganito kasama yung exchange rate natin ngayon.

**[01:40:41.180] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002056)  
And it's bad.

**[01:40:42.340] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002057)  
I think no one can say that.

**[01:40:43.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002058)  
And then yung price ng fuel natin talagang mataas.

**[01:40:51.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002059)  
So, those factors are changing.

**[01:40:55.060] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002060)  
So...

**[01:40:55.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002061)  
So, if let's say all of these things are in place na na-address yung backlog,

**[01:40:59.620] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002062)  
pero God forbid na sobrang tumangit yung ating peso rate,

**[01:41:06.640] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002063)  
then yung pinapredict nila magbabago din.

**[01:41:09.200] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002064)  
I think her honor can understand that kasi there's a lot of...

**[01:41:12.700] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002065)  
Parang commodities yan eh na nagbabago talaga yung price.

**[01:41:15.260] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002066)  
But I think we are one in hoping that with at least the two major issues,

**[01:41:25.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002067)  
efforts of ERC, which is addressing the backlog and the rate reset,

**[01:41:29.800] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002068)  
it's going in the right direction.

**[01:41:31.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002069)  
Dun sa pababa yung presyo.

**[01:41:34.700] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002070)  
I'm beginning to see a pattern here, Mr. President.

**[01:41:38.140] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002071)  
Good sponsor sa sagot ng mga agencies natin na hindi makapagbigay ni ng target

**[01:41:47.760] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002072)  
or reasonable commitment.

**[01:41:53.440] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002073)  
Given...

**[01:41:54.480] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002074)  
Target...

**[01:41:55.320] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002075)  
Target na siya mismo nag-set in the case of DOE nung Setyembre

**[01:41:59.660] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002076)  
or mandate nila sa ilalim ng batas in the case of ERC.

**[01:42:06.500] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002077)  
We could talk a lot also about that rate reset, kung paano mas maayos na ginagawa yan ng ERC.

**[01:42:13.340] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002078)  
But ERC is not as powerless as they say they are.

**[01:42:17.840] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002079)  
May mga kapangyarihan din ng ERC kahit sa ilalim ng EPIRA

**[01:42:23.660] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002080)  
that they could afford to...

**[01:42:25.320] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002081)  
to exercise more assertively and proactively alang-alang sa mga consumers.

**[01:42:33.800] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002082)  
So, niwala ba silang target?

**[01:42:38.740] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002083)  
Kaiba ba ang singil?

**[01:42:40.520] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002084)  
Can they only tell us yung mga external factors na di umano,

**[01:42:45.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002085)  
nag-gumagapo sa kamay nila?

**[01:42:47.960] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002086)  
Given their powers under the EPIRA,

**[01:42:49.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002087)  
are they not even going to try to set a target for next year?

**[01:42:54.820] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002088)  
Mr. President...

**[01:42:55.320] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002089)  
Mr. President, good sponsor.

**[01:43:02.920] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002090)  
Maybe, Mr. President, ERC should also study or explore

**[01:43:06.660] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002091)  
capping, fuel, and forex cost pass-through to consumers.

**[01:43:17.540] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002092)  
Magagawa ba natin ito through regulations?

**[01:43:20.080] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002093)  
O kailangan pa ba intayin yung amendments sa EPIRA para gawin ito?

**[01:43:55.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002095)  
Your Honor, according to ERC, of the total electricity bill,

**[01:44:10.440] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002096)  
what they can regulate is 25% of that.

**[01:44:15.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002097)  
So 75% is beyond their control.

**[01:44:18.980] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002098)  
So in the case of the generation charges,

**[01:44:28.180] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002099)  
if her Honor is suggesting or asking that this be capped,

**[01:44:40.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002100)  
then for ERC, it would be very difficult for them

**[01:44:43.840] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002101)  
to attract investments on that.

**[01:44:48.980] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002102)  
On that side, on the generation side,

**[01:44:50.940] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002103)  
kasi mahirap na yung supplier,

**[01:44:54.060] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002104)  
sasabihin mo hanggang ganito lang,

**[01:44:56.260] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002105)  
pero yung kinukunan niyang...

**[01:45:00.000] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002106)  
main ano niya is so much more expensive because of factors beyond their control like the war, the depreciation rate, that is not beyond their control.

**[01:45:10.700] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002107)  
So kung itatali sila ng ERC, baka huwalang pumayag kasi nga kahit sila, it's beyond their control, Your Honor.

**[01:45:19.320] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002108)  
Well, nabanggit Mr. President ng Good Sponsor yung generation charges.

**[01:45:24.120] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002109)  
Isa yun sa mga tanong na isasubmit ko na lang sa Good Sponsor para sa ERC dahil kung yung system loss charge ay 5 to 10 percent sa bill na pinapason sa consumers,

**[01:45:40.820] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002110)  
yung generation charges ang pinakamalaki, constituting around 60 percent.

**[01:45:45.400] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002111)  
And naka-fold in pa doon yung cost of fuel na hindi pa fully dinidisclose ng lahat.

**[01:45:53.780] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002112)  
So,

**[01:45:54.120] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002113)  
ng mga generation companies.

**[01:45:59.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002114)  
At ang tanong kong isasubmit ko dyan sa Good Sponsor ay tungkol sa pag-review ng mga power supply agreements.

**[01:46:10.220] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002115)  
Huling paksa ko para sa ERC, and ito, pangatlong beses ko na pong itatanong sa ERC, yung pag-audit na mga fuel pass-through ng generation companies nga po.

**[01:46:21.360] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002116)  
Isa sa mga bagay na tinatanong din ang

**[01:46:23.780] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002117)  
ating Senate President.

**[01:46:26.120] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002118)  
May resulta na ba ang review ng ERC?

**[01:46:29.360] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002119)  
Kasi sabi po ng Commission dati na ayaw itong i-disclose ng mga generation companies.

**[01:46:40.640] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002120)  
I can read to you, Your Honor, what was given to me.

**[01:46:45.160] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002121)  
Based on the audit procedure approved by the Commission, 10 DUs and generating companies pairs were identified.

**[01:46:53.640] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002123)  
To be part of the first batch to be subjected to fuel audit, they were selected based on the

**[01:46:58.840] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002124)  
A, size of consumer exposure, this is the DU's total energy sales, and B, magnitude of the fuel cost increase, the blended generation rate billed during the period.

**[01:47:10.520] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002125)  
By assigning equal weight to these criteria, the prioritization methodology ensures that the audit captures the DU-GENCO pairs

**[01:47:17.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002126)  
that not only exhibit significant fuel cost-related increases, but also have the,

**[01:47:23.620] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002128)  
greatest potential impact on consumers, consistent with the objectives of the approved audit procedure.

**[01:47:31.660] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002129)  
The audit team has completed evaluating the documents submitted by one DU-GENCO pair, which was included in the initial batch.

**[01:47:40.100] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002130)  
An exit conference will be scheduled with the said pair before the audit report is finalized.

**[01:47:45.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002131)  
Evaluation of the other GENCO-DU pairs, their submission is ongoing.

**[01:47:50.360] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002132)  
So, may unang pair na.

**[01:47:53.620] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002133)  
So, may walo pa.

**[01:47:54.960] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002134)  
Apat na pairs pa ang pending.

**[01:47:56.700] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002135)  
Mga kailan daw nila matatapos good sponsor yung audit na ito?

**[01:47:59.920] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002136)  
At is nung first batch?

**[01:48:01.440] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002137)  
Ten pairs.

**[01:48:01.900] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002138)  
Ah, siyam.

**[01:48:02.580] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002139)  
So, siyam pa.

**[01:48:03.920] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002140)  
Ten pairs.

**[01:48:04.500] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002141)  
Ten pairs, yes.

**[01:48:05.660] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002142)  
So, may timetable po ba sila para makumpleto itong unang batch na review?

**[01:48:12.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002144)  
By first quarter of next year, Your Honor.

**[01:48:14.940] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002145)  
Ah, alright.

**[01:48:15.660] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002146)  
Soon enough, good sponsor.

**[01:48:18.860] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002147)  
May kakailanganin pa ba sa budget ng ERC sa susunod na taon para,

**[01:48:23.460] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002148)  
para makumpleto ang fuel audit na ito?

**[01:48:26.660] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002149)  
No, they can handle it, Your Honor.

**[01:48:29.660] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002150)  
Alright.

**[01:48:30.780] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002151)  
And kapag nakumpleto na nila ito, first quarter next year,

**[01:48:35.340] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002152)  
pwede po bang magkaroon ng kopya ang kongreso ng mga resultang ito?

**[01:48:39.880] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002153)  
Yes, ang sagot po nila is yes.

**[01:48:41.660] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002154)  
Salamat, good sponsor.

**[01:48:43.300] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002155)  
So, sa pagwawakas ko, Mr. President, good sponsor,

**[01:48:46.780] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002156)  
ulitin ko na lang yung sana naman pagdating ng period of amendments,

**[01:48:51.460] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002157)  
may maisagot na ang...

**[01:48:53.460] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002158)  
Department of Energy.

**[01:48:56.620] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002159)  
Kahit hindi 100%, basta some concrete and realistic commitment

**[01:49:02.780] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002160)  
na sa mga pamilya at households Pilipino,

**[01:49:09.080] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002161)  
lalo na sa Visayas at Mindanao,

**[01:49:11.560] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002162)  
na dumadanas ngayon ng blackouts,

**[01:49:15.280] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002163)  
ilang porsyento sa kanila ay may kasiguruhan na

**[01:49:20.240] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002164)  
kung gusto nila magsindi ng parol ngayong Pasko,

**[01:49:23.460] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002165)  
magagawa nila ito.

**[01:49:25.000] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002166)  
Tulad ng unang tinarget ng department noong September.

**[01:49:28.800] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002167)  
Salamat, Mr. President.

**[01:49:30.400] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-002168)  
Salamat, good sponsor at sa Dio.

**[01:49:32.220] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002169)  
Thank you, po.

**[01:49:32.920] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002170)  
Thank you.

**[01:49:33.300] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002171)  
Thank you, Senator Riza Hontiveros.

**[01:49:36.720] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002172)  
Next will be Senator Aimee Marcos.

**[01:49:39.020] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002173)  
Maraming salamat.

**[01:49:40.320] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002174)  
At hindi naman akong mag-interpolate as such,

**[01:49:43.020] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002175)  
kundi sasabihin lamang na sa ating chairwoman at sa ating sekretary,

**[01:49:48.560] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002176)  
pati na rin sa ating president,

**[01:49:50.720] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002177)  
napaka-late na, mag-alas dos na,

**[01:49:53.300] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002178)  
talagang naglalampumpusa na tayo.

**[01:49:55.780] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002179)  
Ang akin na lamang,

**[01:49:56.760] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002180)  
this isn't the first time we've had an oil price shock.

**[01:49:59.320] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002181)  
We went through it in 1974 and 1979,

**[01:50:03.060] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002182)  
and without declaring that I'm the oldest person in the room

**[01:50:06.360] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002183)  
who remembers all this,

**[01:50:08.680] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002184)  
marami naman tayong ginawa noon

**[01:50:10.240] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002185)  
na maari pa siguro natin ulitin ngayon,

**[01:50:13.580] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002186)  
na hindi pa natin nagagawa.

**[01:50:15.880] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002187)  
At dahil nga,

**[01:50:17.400] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002188)  
tayo ang pinakamataas sa Southeast Asia,

**[01:50:19.500] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002189)  
15 to 16 pesos sa Metro Manila area,

**[01:50:22.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002190)  
per kilowatt.

**[01:50:24.220] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002191)  
Ang bigat noon, it's so high,

**[01:50:25.860] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002192)  
in all of ASEAN, we're the highest.

**[01:50:28.720] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002193)  
So clearly, our neighbors are doing things that we had done before,

**[01:50:33.920] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002194)  
and that might work.

**[01:50:35.260] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002195)  
So maraming nagtatanong kung open ang DOE sa iba't-ibang suggestion.

**[01:50:42.120] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002196)  
Dahil hindi po namin nakikita sa budget ang ganito.

**[01:50:47.280] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002197)  
Unang-una, yung fixing the outdated power supply agreements,

**[01:50:51.640] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002198)  
yung mga PSA,

**[01:50:52.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002199)  
yung mga legacy agreements,

**[01:50:55.900] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002200)  
nasaksakan ng mahal.

**[01:50:57.760] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002201)  
Nasa 70% ng Meralco,

**[01:51:00.160] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002202)  
they're still tied to very old, expensive contracts sa Ilocos Norte.

**[01:51:04.420] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002203)  
Nakatali rin kami dyan sa mga legacy-legacy contracts na yan.

**[01:51:09.940] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002204)  
Wala bang posibilidad na isuspenso o magdeklarasyon ang DOE?

**[01:51:16.540] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002205)  
Nateka muna, pwede naman siguro huminahon muna dito

**[01:51:20.300] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002206)  
sa mga legacy contracts at renegotiation.

**[01:51:23.280] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002207)  
Anong opinion ni Secretary at ni Chair dyan?

**[01:51:27.480] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002208)  
They're still tied to all these old, expensive contracts reliant on fossil fuels

**[01:51:32.540] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002209)  
na para sa amin, sana naging renewable na dahil marami namang kaming renewable sa Ilocos.

**[01:51:39.720] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002210)  
Pero we're stuck with those contracts, 10 years.

**[01:51:43.160] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002211)  
Meralco, 70% legacy contract.

**[01:51:46.260] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002212)  
Pwede bang tignan yun?

**[01:51:47.400] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002213)  
Suggestion lang po.

**[01:51:48.800] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002214)  
Tumatangong si Secretary, baka pwede naman isuspend,

**[01:51:51.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002215)  
i-postpone.

**[01:51:53.600] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002216)  
Yes, they are willing to look into it, Your Honor.

**[01:51:56.940] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002217)  
Yes, and then another thing is,

**[01:51:59.720] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002218)  
pag nagkaroon ng gulo na sa WESEM,

**[01:52:03.620] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002219)  
is it possible that we enforce competitive bidding?

**[01:52:07.980] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002220)  
Yung competitive selection process for the brand new contracts that we will embark upon?

**[01:52:13.880] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002221)  
And then remove the automatic fuel pass-through clauses

**[01:52:17.500] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002222)  
that insulate the power generators from global price hikes?

**[01:52:21.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002223)  
Na tayo lahat ang nag-aabsorb.

**[01:52:23.980] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002224)  
It could significantly lower baseline rates.

**[01:52:28.680] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002225)  
Diba?

**[01:52:29.280] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002226)  
Kasi magiging competitive bidding na yan eh.

**[01:52:31.560] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002227)  
Pagalingan na yan.

**[01:52:32.940] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002228)  
And then yung automatic fuel pass-through.

**[01:52:36.180] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002229)  
Tanggal na lahat dyan.

**[01:52:37.760] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002230)  
So that's one consideration also.

**[01:52:42.160] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002231)  
Anong tingin ninyo?

**[01:52:43.280] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002232)  
Is that something we can consider?

**[01:52:45.000] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002233)  
They're already implementing and looking into this competitive selection, Your Honor.

**[01:52:50.800] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002234)  
Oh, that's great.

**[01:52:51.560] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002235)  
Kasi I don't see it in the line items of the budgets.

**[01:52:55.040] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002236)  
Pero siguro nandyan sa operation costs naman ninyo, no?

**[01:52:58.280] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002237)  
Nagagawin nyo.

**[01:52:59.060] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002238)  
As long as there's a commitment to that.

**[01:53:01.300] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002239)  
Kasama na sa policy, Your Honor.

**[01:53:03.040] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002240)  
Oh, that's great.

**[01:53:03.900] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002241)  
So that's very good news.

**[01:53:05.940] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002242)  
And sana tignan ninyo nga yung mga legacy contracts

**[01:53:08.780] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002243)  
that are burdening so many electric cooperatives,

**[01:53:11.840] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002244)  
including Meralko itself.

**[01:53:13.700] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002245)  
And the other aspect that we could embark upon,

**[01:53:17.400] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002246)  
I don't know, this is a suggestion from an old energy hand,

**[01:53:21.560] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002247)  
is to clear the transmission and grid bottlenecks.

**[01:53:26.020] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002248)  
Under the EPIRA, transmission capacity has simply failed to keep pace

**[01:53:30.020] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002249)  
with power generation.

**[01:53:31.640] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002250)  
Alam natin yan, yung ibang transmission na dekada 70 pa yan,

**[01:53:36.100] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002251)  
bulok na bulok na, no?

**[01:53:37.080] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002252)  
So we have to accelerate the grid upgrades

**[01:53:41.280] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002253)  
and improve power plant efficiency.

**[01:53:45.640] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002254)  
May existing power plants that are only operating

**[01:53:48.280] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002255)  
and the secretary, I've heard you talk about this,

**[01:53:51.560] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002256)  
they're only at 50% efficiency and capacity.

**[01:53:55.640] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002257)  
Maswerte ka na kung 80%.

**[01:53:57.940] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002258)  
So hindi ko alam kung luma ba yan

**[01:54:00.420] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002259)  
or kung ano ba yung problema nila.

**[01:54:04.140] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002260)  
Pero yun na naman, wala na naman sa budget,

**[01:54:07.260] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002261)  
kaya kinakabahan ako,

**[01:54:09.100] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002262)  
kailan ba maa-upgrade yung aging infrastructure

**[01:54:12.000] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002263)  
aside from transmission

**[01:54:13.420] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002264)  
so that we can minimize the unplanned outages

**[01:54:16.760] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002265)  
and stabilize supply, prevent sudden price hikes

**[01:54:20.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002266)  
and sudden outages.

**[01:54:21.540] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002267)  
Mahal yun.

**[01:54:24.840] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002268)  
I know it's expensive, yung mga power plant efficiency, di ba?

**[01:54:32.980] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002269)  
Your Honor, the response is ERC has already been approving

**[01:54:38.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002270)  
the submitted proposals for CAPEX of NGCP.

**[01:54:42.800] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002271)  
Ah, that's great.

**[01:54:43.460] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002272)  
So we have that to look forward to.

**[01:54:44.900] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002273)  
Yes, and then in addition, doon naman sa dagdag niyong question,

**[01:54:47.620] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002274)  
is for government,

**[01:54:51.540] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002275)  
to be allowed to...

**[01:54:53.080] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002276)  
For talking to NGCP, to allow government...

**[01:54:55.780] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002277)  
To allow government to directly fund or...

**[01:54:59.880] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002278)  
Undertake the transmission.

**[01:55:02.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002279)  
Okay, so for government to undertake the transmission.

**[01:55:05.320] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002280)  
Yes, inasmuch as they haven't done anything about it,

**[01:55:09.020] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002281)  
they haven't modernized, they haven't invested,

**[01:55:11.880] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002282)  
I think government simply needs to take over.

**[01:55:14.500] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002283)  
I have also heard the Secretary talk about establishing our own refineries.

**[01:55:20.380] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002284)  
Right now, we're stuck.

**[01:55:21.540] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002285)  
We're stuck with one privately owned refinery.

**[01:55:24.860] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002286)  
Alam na natin yun.

**[01:55:26.420] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002287)  
Dati-dati, may tatlong refinery.

**[01:55:29.420] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002288)  
Ano ba ang posisyon ng DOE?

**[01:55:32.020] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002289)  
Kasi wala rin sa budget ninyo, plano or FS sa refinery.

**[01:55:36.660] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002290)  
Ano sa tingin nyo?

**[01:55:37.540] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002291)  
Kasi I heard the Secretary, parang naganahan kami, nabuhayan kami nun eh.

**[01:55:51.540] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002292)  
I mean, obviously, that's a very, very big investment.

**[01:55:59.480] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002293)  
Ma'am?

**[01:56:06.260] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002294)  
Your Honor, PNOC currently, and they do have a budget for it,

**[01:56:11.260] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002295)  
the first step is the storage facility.

**[01:56:14.240] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002296)  
That's right.

**[01:56:16.380] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002297)  
And then, the second step is more storage.

**[01:56:19.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002298)  
And then, they are now in...

**[01:56:21.540] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002299)  
They're in talks with...

**[01:56:23.160] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002300)  
Did you say Middle East?

**[01:56:24.160] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002301)  
Middle East, other Western countries.

**[01:56:27.980] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002302)  
And according to Trump.

**[01:56:30.760] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002303)  
Yes.

**[01:56:31.740] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002304)  
They're in talks with...

**[01:56:33.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002305)  
Wala na, [unclear] na.

**[01:56:36.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002306)  
They're in talks for refineries, Mr. Your Honor.

**[01:56:42.060] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002307)  
Actually, that's the other thing that I was going to inquire about.

**[01:56:45.220] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002308)  
Deploying utility-scale battery storage.

**[01:56:48.260] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002309)  
Expanding the battery energy storage systems.

**[01:56:51.060] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002310)  
Yung BESS.

**[01:56:51.540] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002315)  
Across the country, 7,000 islands to stabilize intermittent wind and solar

**[01:56:57.580] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002316)  
and cut reliance on expensive diesel peaker plants.

**[01:57:02.220] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002317)  
That was another consideration, no?

**[01:57:05.760] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002318)  
Yung utility-scale battery storage.

**[01:57:09.020] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002319)  
Hinahanap ko rin kasi sa budget ninyo yan.

**[01:57:11.360] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002320)  
Kasi eto yung mga structural bottlenecks, quite apart from basic household conservation, no?

**[01:57:18.960] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002321)  
Where we can bring down the cost.

**[01:57:21.540] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002322)  
So, your honor, they have policies on best for the storage.

**[01:57:31.320] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002323)  
Pero kailangan natin bilhin yung battery.

**[01:57:35.220] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002324)  
Sa private pa rin.

**[01:57:37.800] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002325)  
Sa private pa rin.

**[01:57:38.740] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002326)  
Tapos yung requirement nila...

**[01:57:39.680] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002327)  
So, is there interest?

**[01:57:40.620] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002328)  
May mag-iinvest dyan?

**[01:57:41.920] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002329)  
Yes, ma'am.

**[01:57:42.740] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002330)  
Meron naman daw.

**[01:57:43.520] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002331)  
The requirement pa nga is that for a project, 20% of it has battery storage.

**[01:57:50.440] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002332)  
Which?

**[01:57:51.380] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002333)  
Which brings me to another topic, and that's to remove the red tape on rooftop and distributed solar.

**[01:57:58.840] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002334)  
Kasi ang dami-daming red tape, ang dami-daming pirma, wala na si Mig Subiri, isa yung nagre-reklamo lagi.

**[01:58:06.420] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002335)  
How do we streamline permitting and net metering?

**[01:58:10.360] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002336)  
The long approval times, o ayan, kasali na LGU dyan, hindi naman lahat kasalanan DOE.

**[01:58:17.480] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002337)  
And the distribution utilities, they simply discourage...

**[01:58:21.380] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002338)  
home owners and businesses from installing solar.

**[01:58:25.260] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002339)  
Nakakapagod ikot-ikotin lahat ng opisyal at magpapirma ng sagkatutak na papel.

**[01:58:32.100] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002340)  
And even with the new legislative push, yung zero export solar bills natin,

**[01:58:39.340] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002341)  
the proposal to cut down on renewable hardware costs,

**[01:58:43.200] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002342)  
ang dami-daming proposal, pero kapag ganong kabagal yung red tape,

**[01:58:48.420] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002343)  
paano ba itulak yung mga LGU?

**[01:58:50.560] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002344)  
Dapat ba i-penalize yan, or yung mga utilities, kailangan ba may sanction para kumilos?

**[01:58:59.440] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002345)  
On that last point, na ang dami-dami, matagal, so ARTA is now involved.

**[01:59:04.760] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002346)  
Yes.

**[01:59:05.260] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002347)  
Because that really is the goal, to simplify it.

**[01:59:08.340] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002348)  
So, to distinguish, when there is no net metering involved,

**[01:59:12.640] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002349)  
then wala nang mga iba-iba pang permits, they just have to inform the DOE.

**[01:59:18.680] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002350)  
Oh, that's perfect.

**[01:59:20.560] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002351)  
Ipahihwating natin sa marami.

**[01:59:22.740] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002352)  
Kasi marami talaga napuput off dahil ang hirap-hirap.

**[01:59:26.500] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002353)  
So, tinatamad na sila.

**[01:59:28.060] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002354)  
The other thing, of course, is that we are always being told that there's millions in green financing.

**[01:59:34.100] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002355)  
But to unlock state-backed financing sa pag-ibig, sa SSS, sa GSIS,

**[01:59:40.980] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002356)  
for low-interest microloans, for residential solar and battery setups,

**[01:59:46.640] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002357)  
it needs a real push.

**[01:59:50.000] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002358)  
Kasi...

**[01:59:50.560] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002359)  
We should utilize these institutions.

**[01:59:53.060] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002360)  
Andyan na yan eh.

**[01:59:54.200] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002361)  
Nag-aawas na sila sa sweldo eh.

**[01:59:57.940] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002362)  
Ah, so wala pa?

**[01:59:59.140] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002363)  
Wala pa.

**[01:59:59.640] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002364)  
Thank you.

**[02:00:00.000] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002365)  
Tapos magmuha na tayo dyan sa mga pag-ibig, yung mga yan.

**[02:00:06.100] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002366)  
Kasi may pera na yan eh.

**[02:00:07.600] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002367)  
Meron silang energy loan.

**[02:00:09.060] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002368)  
Meron.

**[02:00:09.480] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002369)  
Pero what we want is they promote it.

**[02:00:11.340] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002370)  
Okay.

**[02:00:12.200] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002371)  
So, there is like a GSIS and is it also?

**[02:00:16.880] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002372)  
Yeah, GSIS, SSS, pag-ibig.

**[02:00:19.640] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002373)  
Pag-ibig loan.

**[02:00:19.940] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002374)  
But they are meeting along with the executive secretary, these agencies, to really promote it.

**[02:00:27.460] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002375)  
Para ma-push talaga ang pag-ibig.

**[02:00:29.820] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002376)  
Hindi nila inoobligay yan eh.

**[02:00:31.360] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002377)  
They're not offering it as an option.

**[02:00:33.960] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002378)  
That there should be low interest, micro loans.

**[02:00:36.720] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002379)  
Pwede kang mag-solar para sa business mo, para sa bahay mo.

**[02:00:41.020] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002380)  
Hindi nila ino-offer.

**[02:00:42.840] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002381)  
And they're working also with the provincial governments to, so that on the local, on the ground.

**[02:00:50.580] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002382)  
And then makakapagbigay pa ng trabaho yan.

**[02:00:53.280] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002383)  
Dapat hilain rin yung TESDA at yung mga trade and training institutions.

**[02:00:59.820] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002384)  
Para silang magturo ng scaling up local installation labor.

**[02:01:05.680] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002385)  
You will generate lots of jobs just putting up all those solar panels and so on.

**[02:01:10.820] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002386)  
Dapat mag-training rin yung TESDA para sabay-sabay.

**[02:01:14.180] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002387)  
And then you will successfully unlock all that government financing.

**[02:01:22.660] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002388)  
Okay.

**[02:01:23.160] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002389)  
So, they're currently doing trainings, as her honor mentioned, and accreditations.

**[02:01:26.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002390)  
The secretary agrees with that.

**[02:01:29.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002391)  
She agrees with everything that the, the, our honorable senator from Ilocos said.

**[02:01:36.160] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002392)  
Thank you very much.

**[02:01:37.180] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002393)  
Which brings me to Ilocos.

**[02:01:39.000] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002394)  
And that's to unblock all the stalled renewable energy projects.

**[02:01:44.640] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002395)  
That's been a real problem.

**[02:01:46.780] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002396)  
Ang tagal-tagal, no?

**[02:01:48.040] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002397)  
The transition from imported coal and gas is moving much slower than planned.

**[02:01:54.100] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002398)  
I looked at the budget for RE and it's business as usual.

**[02:01:57.860] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002399)  
We know we're in panic mode.

**[02:01:59.820] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002400)  
It's not business as usual.

**[02:02:02.280] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002401)  
We have to push and enforce the auction timelines.

**[02:02:06.300] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002402)  
Ang liwanag ng timeline but less than 20% conform with those timelines.

**[02:02:11.680] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002403)  
So, the government's first two green energy auctions have been completed but less than 20%.

**[02:02:19.260] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002404)  
Bakit ganun kababa yun?

**[02:02:21.960] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002405)  
The majority are facing delays.

**[02:02:23.900] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002406)  
80% delay, suspension, right of way, permitting.

**[02:02:29.820] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002407)  
Ang mga yung tight, o.

**[02:02:31.740] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002408)  
Uh, one and two.

**[02:02:34.600] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002409)  
Anong mga may face, one and two?

**[02:02:53.680] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002421)  
So, uh, your honor, yung Green Energy Auction 1 and 2

**[02:02:57.600] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002422)  
admittedly mabagal talaga.

**[02:02:59.580] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002423)  
Yes sir.

**[02:02:59.760] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002424)  
So they've corrected this and for 3 and 4, they're succeeding.

**[02:03:08.160] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002425)  
Excited kasi kami dyan. Akala namin talagang yun na yung sagot.

**[02:03:11.800] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002426)  
Kaso 20% lang ang successful eh. Kaya the hurdles really are structural and need to be overcome.

**[02:03:19.360] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002427)  
Finally, ito is not entirely a DOE. But at the end of the day, kayo rin ang sasagot ng pambayad.

**[02:03:25.760] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002428)  
And that is modernizing the cold chain and industrial efficiency.

**[02:03:30.640] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002429)  
We've been obsessing about headline inflation and of course they encompass by and large food costs as well as the rise in energy prices.

**[02:03:39.800] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002430)  
So overhauling agricultural logistics is very clearly part of that.

**[02:03:45.340] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002431)  
And they all rely, all these cold storage facilities of VA, of DAR, they rely very heavily on expensive grid electronics.

**[02:03:55.760] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002432)  
Electricity and crude oil, diesel, which is now even more expensive than gasoline.

**[02:04:01.940] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002433)  
So scaling up energy efficient, climate smart cold chain hubs like those piloted under the National Cold Storage Industry Roadmap.

**[02:04:13.320] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002434)  
Na palagay ko DOE is also part of that planning.

**[02:04:17.120] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002435)  
Would reduce all that energy waste and stabilize the peak grid demand.

**[02:04:23.560] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002436)  
Alam naman natin may timing.

**[02:04:25.760] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002437)  
Ayan eh. At pwede naman siguro i-adjust.

**[02:04:46.520] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002444)  
Your Honor, there is a fuel transition plan.

**[02:04:55.760] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002445)  
To really lower the dependence on fossil fuel.

**[02:04:58.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002446)  
So that's in place.

**[02:04:59.460] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002447)  
And they're working closely with DA to...

**[02:05:06.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002448)  
For the solarization project.

**[02:05:11.180] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002449)  
To...

**[02:05:12.240] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002450)  
Solarization to...

**[02:05:13.780] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002451)  
Yes.

**[02:05:14.400] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002452)  
To transition.

**[02:05:16.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002453)  
That's right.

**[02:05:17.260] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002454)  
Right now the solar irrigation is being distributed by both DA and NIA.

**[02:05:21.960] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002455)  
And the question now is...

**[02:05:25.760] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002456)  
What is the best situation with the cold storage?

**[02:05:29.180] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002457)  
Kasi yung logistics chain very heavily dependent and very, very gasoline and diesel consuming.

**[02:05:39.960] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002458)  
If there's a way for us to transition that quickly, we will be able to bring both food costs as well as energy costs down in one go.

**[02:06:27.580] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002473)  
So, Your Honor, they have discussions with NIA to put together a green energy auction for their water assets.

**[02:06:48.580] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002474)  
Oh, that's great for the cold storage and other logistics facilities.

**[02:06:52.580] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002475)  
Magiging green auction rin kayo?

**[02:06:56.580] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002477)  
For like the irrigation.

**[02:06:57.580] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002478)  
Irrigation canals and the dams.

**[02:06:59.580] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002479)  
Ah, okay.

**[02:06:59.920] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002479-split-2)  
Yes, that solar irrigation is in place. It's the post-harvest now that's costing so much.

**[02:07:19.580] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002482)  
So, sa DA naman, they're talking to them so that yung cold storage nila would be using renewable energy.

**[02:07:26.580] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002483)  
So, they're discussing that with them to help them make that transition.

**[02:07:32.580] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002484)  
Thank you so much for being open to all these suggestions.

**[02:07:37.580] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002485)  
As I said, this isn't our first rodeo.

**[02:07:41.580] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002486)  
We've been through this before.

**[02:07:43.580] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002487)

**[02:07:54.580] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002488)  
And of course, we in the legislature.

**[02:07:55.580] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002489)  
Of course, we in the legislature will be very happy to support and legislate as necessary.

**[02:08:02.580] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002490)  
Maraming salamat.

**[02:08:03.580] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002491)  
Thank you.

**[02:08:04.580] Senator Imee R. Marcos** (asr-002492)  
At inaasahan ko na bababa ang presyo.

**[02:08:06.580] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-002493)  
Yes. Salamat for all your interventions, Your Honor.

**[02:08:23.580] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002496)  
Kaya

**[02:08:24.580] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002497)

**[02:08:40.580] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002500)

**[02:08:49.580] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002501)

**[02:08:52.580] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002502)  
meron pinakamababang economic growth.

**[02:08:57.580] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002503)  
Because mataas ang presyo ng kuryente natin.

**[02:09:00.580] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002504)

**[02:09:05.580] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002505)  
Kahit na anong roadshow natin sa buong mundo, kahit anong incentives ang ibibigay natin kung mataas ang presyo ng kuryente natin,

**[02:09:13.580] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002506)  
walang papasok at papasok sa ating bansa.

**[02:09:16.580] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002507)  
So, this is a challenge to the DOE.

**[02:09:21.580] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002508)  
Especially the ERC.

**[02:09:23.580] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002509)  
Tama ho si Sen. Risa, trabaho to ng ERC.

**[02:09:27.580] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002510)  
At next year with your new budget, we urge you to focus on how to bring down electricity rates.

**[02:09:36.580] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002511)  
Hindi walang pag-asa ho tayo.

**[02:09:38.580] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002512)  
In fact, tama po yung sinabi ni Sen. Aimee, maraming over-contracting, maraming legacy PSAs, mabagal tayo mag-reset ang WAC natin,

**[02:09:49.580] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002513)  
double digit figures.

**[02:09:50.580] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002514)  
Hindi walang pag-asa eh.

**[02:09:54.580] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002515)  
Hindi lang tayo determinadong ibaba ang presyo ng kuryente.

**[02:09:59.580] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002516)  
So, ito po yung hamon namin sa ERC.

**[02:10:02.580] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002517)  
Dati malaki ang tiwala ko sa ERC pero para ngayon, nawawalan na ako ng tiwala na bababa ang presyo ng kuryente natin dahil meron tayong effective na regulator.

**[02:10:16.580] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002518)  
So, with that, we have a new budget. We have a new year.

**[02:10:20.580] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002519)  
Ang challenge namin sa ERC and also to the DOE is to lower down our electricity rates.

**[02:10:27.580] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002520)  
So, maraming salamat, Majority Leader.
